Each video includes an executable event trace for capability-surface estimation and timestamp-level evaluation. Our results reveal a staged temporal failure. At an 80% reliability threshold, Gemini 3.6 Flash reliably counts persistent state transitions up to 12 events at 0.5 and 1.0 Hz, yet demonstrates no reliable positive-count region for transient blinking events. Thus, event representation dictates whether a model initially accesses evidence -- a limitation that compounds as count and frequency increase. In the high-count, high-frequency regime, only 0.2% of final counts are correct and the model recovers just 18.1% of true events. To test if visual access is the primary bottleneck, we increase sampling rate. Although this boosts Bounce Ball accuracy from 19.6% to 29.3%, the reported sequence agrees with ground truth only 3.7% of the time. Extra frames can therefore inflate final scores without producing faithful event recovery. Different prompting strategies yield similarly limited gains, and real-world video evaluations show the same concentration of success at low event counts. Ultimately, trace-grounded profiling shifts video evaluation from aggregate accuracy metrics to a detailed diagnostic of where temporal reasoning fails.

</details>


### [134] [Tracing the Heart: An Evidence-Linked Pipeline for Heart-Failure Feature Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06366)
*Soorya Ram Shimgekar,Michelle Hu,Dorisa Shehi,Daniel Kang,Roy Ka-Wei Lee,Koustuv Saha,Christian Poellabauer,Christopher Lee,Sajeev Singh,Piyum Zonooz,Navin Kumar,Zeeshan Ahmed,Priyadarshini Kachroo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Nimblemind多智能体系统（nMAS），用于基于证据和评分标准自动完成心力衰竭电子健康记录特征工程，并在模拟患者数据上验证其结构完整性、可追溯性及对表型识别性能的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子健康记录特征工程占据数据科学家大量工作时间，心力衰竭研究还需整合碎片化数据与疾病特异性、指南驱动的临床推理。现有规则系统和大语言模型方法在自动化程度、可维护性及证据追踪方面仍有限。

Method: 构建证据关联、评分标准驱动的nMAS流程，处理来自9个电子健康记录源表的500条模拟患者记录，生成132个结构化聚合特征和70个基于评分标准的聚合特征，并通过结构完整性、评分标准符合性、来源追踪及受限大语言模型审计进行验证。使用加入聚合特征前后的模型性能比较评估其对HFrEF和HFpEF表型识别的影响，同时采用独立的大语言模型评分证据支持度与方法学严谨性。

Result: 加入聚合特征后，HFrEF表型识别的留出集AUROC由0.895提升至0.963，HFpEF由0.870提升至0.910。特征在证据支持度和方法学合理性方面获得独立评分的81.5%。生成特征通过了结构、规范符合性和来源验证。

Conclusion: nMAS展示了在复杂心血管电子健康记录中实现自动化、可审计特征工程的可行性，并能提升心力衰竭表型识别性能。但研究仅基于单机构模拟队列，尚需在真实、多机构数据上进行外部验证，并进一步评估其泛化性与临床实用性。

Abstract: Electronic health record (EHR) feature engineering is a major bottleneck in clinical research and AI, accounting for 39-45% of data scientists' workload. This is especially pronounced in heart failure, which affects an estimated 6.7 million U.S. adults and requires integrating fragmented EHR data with disease-specific, guideline-based clinical reasoning. Existing rule-based and large language model (LLM)-based approaches offer only partial automation with limited maintainability and evidence traceability. We developed the Nimblemind Multi-Agent System (nMAS), an evidence-linked, rubric-grounded pipeline for automated heart-failure feature engineering, and evaluated it on 500 dummy patient records from nine EHR source tables. nMAS generated 132 structured and 70 rubric-scored aggregated features, verified for structural integrity, rubric compliance, and provenance, and audited by a restricted LLM. Adding the aggregated features improved held-out AUROC from 0.895 to 0.963 for HFrEF and 0.870 to 0.910 for HFpEF phenotyping, and an independent LLM-based rubric assessment of evidence support and methodological soundness scored the features at 81.5% of maximum points. These results demonstrate the feasibility of automated, auditable feature engineering for complex cardiovascular EHR data, though evaluation was limited to a single-institution cohort and external validation is needed.

</details>


<div id='cs.LO'></div>

# cs.LO [[Back]](#toc)

### [135] [Bit-Precise CHC Satisfiability Using Theory-Modular Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05337)
*Omer Rappoport,Orna Grumberg,Yakir Vizel*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: Mosaic是一个面向定长位向量理论的约束Horn子句可满足性判定框架，通过协同使用位向量算术与整数算术，提高位精确程序验证中的求解性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CHC求解器在定长位向量理论上的可扩展性较弱，难以有效处理位级操作相关的程序验证问题，因此需要一种能够结合不同理论优势的模块化求解方法。

Method: Mosaic将输入的定长位向量CHC划分为分别在位向量理论和整数算术理论下解释的两个片段；随后以模块化方式分别推理，并通过理论之间的可靠转换交换信息，最终判定原始CHC集合的可满足性。原型系统基于Z3和Spacer实现。

Result: 在位操作基准测试上的实验表明，Mosaic相较于Spacer取得了显著的性能提升，说明跨理论协同能够改善定长位向量CHC的求解效率。摘要未提供具体测试规模、运行时间或提升比例。

Conclusion: 该工作提出了一种理论模块化的定长位向量CHC求解框架，验证了将位向量推理与整数算术推理结合的有效性，为提升位精确程序验证的可扩展性提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Deciding satisfiability of Constrained Horn Clauses (CHCs) modulo the theory of fixed-size bit-vectors ($\mathcal{T}_B$) is fundamental to bit-precise program verification. However, state-of-the-art CHC-solvers often struggle with $\mathcal{T}_B$, limiting scalability in bit-precise reasoning. We present Mosaic, a theory-modular framework for deciding satisfiability of CHCs modulo $\mathcal{T}_B$ by combining reasoning in $\mathcal{T}_B$ and the theory of Integer Arithmetic ($\mathcal{T}_I$). Given a CHC set modulo $\mathcal{T}_B$, Mosaic partitions it into two fragments interpreted over $\mathcal{T}_B$ and $\mathcal{T}_I$. Moreover, it implements an algorithm that reasons about the fragments in a modular fashion, exchanges information between them via sound translations across theories, and determines satisfiability w.r.t. the original CHC set. We implemented a prototype of Mosaic using Z3 and Spacer and evaluated it on bit-manipulating benchmarks. Our evaluation shows that Mosaic significantly outperforms Spacer on these benchmarks.

</details>


### [136] [Can Open-Weight LLMs Produce Kernel-Verified Coq Proofs? A Pilot Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05420)
*Ahmed Ryan,Md Erfan,Akond Ashfaque Ur Rahman,Md Rayhanur Rahman*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 该试点研究在CoqStoq的100个定理上评估了6个开放权重大语言模型，共进行600次证明尝试，只有21次被Coq内核验证通过，总体成功率为3.5%。Gemma 4表现最好，验证12个定理，但研究未进行统计显著性检验，不能据此建立模型的普遍排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的文本可能具有数学证明的表面形式，但只有形式化证明检查器能够确认其逻辑正确性。研究旨在测量不同LLM在真实Coq项目环境中生成可被内核接受的形式证明的能力。

Method: 研究选取CoqStoq中的100个定理，评估6个开放权重LLM。每个模型对每个定理仅生成一次证明，温度设为0，并在定理原始项目环境中由Coq检查。只有被Coq内核接受的证明才计为成功。同时记录模型、定理、参考证明长度及生成成本，并与标准Coq策略基线进行比较。

Result: Gemma 4验证12个定理，Llama 3.3验证8个，DeepSeek Coder V2 Lite验证1个；Qwen 3.5、Mistral Small 3.1和GPT-OSS均未成功。21次成功结果涉及15个不同定理，其中11个未被标准Coq策略基线解决。所有成功定理的人工参考证明均为短或中等长度，没有模型验证长参考证明对应的定理。对有成功记录的3个模型而言，每个已验证证明的生成成本为741至36193个输出词元、14.9至178.0秒和0.0167至0.2000个GPU小时。

Conclusion: 在该基准、单次采样和原始环境约束下，LLM自动生成可被Coq内核验证的证明仍较困难，但部分模型能够解决标准策略未解决的定理。证明长度与成功率之间的关系仅为探索性观察，不能推断因果关系；由于未进行统计检验，结果也不能证明六个模型之间存在普遍性能排名。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate text that resembles a mathematical proof, but resemblance does not establish correctness. A formal proof checker verifies whether each proof step follows established logical rules. Coq bases its rules on the Calculus of Inductive Constructions, a logical framework that defines which proof steps the system may accept.
  This pilot study evaluated six open-weight LLMs on the same 100 theorems from CoqStoq, a benchmark derived from real Coq projects. Each LLM received one attempt per theorem with the temperature set to 0, and Coq checked every proposed proof in the theorem's original project environment. We counted a proof as successful only if the Coq kernel accepted it.
  Gemma 4 verified 12 of 100 theorems, Llama 3.3 verified 8, and DeepSeek Coder V2 Lite verified 1. Qwen 3.5, Mistral Small 3.1, and GPT-OSS verified none. The 21 successful model-theorem results covered 15 distinct theorems, 11 of which were not solved by a baseline of standard Coq tactics. All verified theorems had short or medium human-written reference proofs; no model verified a theorem with a long reference proof. Because the proof-length analysis was exploratory, this pattern does not establish that proof length caused the difference.
  For the three models with at least one success, the total generation cost per verified proof ranged from 741 to 36,193 output tokens, 14.9 to 178.0 seconds, and 0.0167 to 0.2000 aggregate GPU hours. We could not calculate these ratios for models with no verified proofs. Across 600 attempts, the models produced 21 kernel-verified proofs, giving an overall success rate of 3.5%. The study reports descriptive differences among the models but does not statistically test whether one model outperforms another. Therefore, the results do not establish a universal ranking of the six models.

</details>


### [137] [A Bitopological Approach to Finite Reduction and Bounded Exact-Value Certificates for Fitting's Finite Heyting-valued Modal Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05550)
*Litan Kumar Das,Kumar Sankar Ray,Prakash Chandra Mali*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文研究有限Heyting值模态逻辑中的有限状态约简与精确值保持问题，证明观测商与双拓扑对偶中的有限像同构，并构造深度受模态深度约束的有限树状证书。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限Heyting值模态模型可能包含冗余状态，传统约简需要确保不仅保留公式真假，还保留其精确真值。因此需要一种有限、可计算且具有最小性的状态约简方法，以及对公式失败进行有限见证的方法。

Method: 利用关系双拓扑表示，将有限模型和有限词汇下由原子赋值生成的模态子代数编码为状态—评价映射。基于该映射构造观测商，并证明其与双拓扑对偶中的有限像对应；同时通过递归构造有限树状精确值证书，控制证书深度和分支规模。

Result: 观测商同构于双拓扑对偶中的有限像，商关系正是规范对偶关系的限制。所有给定词汇上的公式都保持精确真值，且该商是所有生成观测可因子化的满射约简中最小的。对任意公式和状态，都存在深度不超过模态深度的有限树状精确值证书，其分支规模仅依赖于真值代数的高度和盒算子子公式数量。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了有限Heyting值模态逻辑的精确有限状态约简理论，并证明失败公式具有有界规模的约简反例，从而为模型压缩、可判定性分析和精确语义验证提供了结构化工具。

Abstract: Fitting's finite Heyting-valued modal logic interprets modal formulas
  over a finite Heyting algebra. We use a relational bitopological representation to obtain a finite-state reduction. For a finite model and a finite vocabulary, the modal subalgebra generated by the atomic valuations determines a state-evaluation map. We prove that the
  observational quotient is isomorphic to its finite image in the
  bitopological dual and that the quotient relation is the restriction
  of the canonical dual relation. Hence every formula over the
  vocabulary preserves its exact truth value, and the quotient is
  minimal among surjective reductions through which all generated
  observations factor. In addition, for any formula and state, we
  construct a finite tree-like exact-value certificate whose depth is
  bounded by modal depth and whose branching depends only on the height
  of the truth-value algebra and the number of boxed subformulas. Failed
  formulas therefore admit bounded reduced counterexamples preserving
  their precise failure values.

</details>


### [138] [Implicit Computation of Filtered Prime Implicants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05943)
*Edward Liem,Clemens Dubslaff*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出面向受约束输入空间的素蕴含项（PI）符号化计算方法，避免先计算全部PI再进行过滤所导致的指数级开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PI算法主要针对完整输入空间；当输入受到上下文或结构依赖约束时，现有方法通常先生成所有PI，再执行显式后处理过滤。由于PI数量可能呈指数增长，该流程存在严重的可扩展性问题。

Method: 扩展基于决策图的Coudert–Madre方法，设计端到端的符号化算法，使满足侧约束的PI集合能够被隐式表示。同时实现模块化工具链，将决策图构建、PI计算和约束过滤三个阶段解耦。

Result: 所提出的方法能够在PI计算过程中直接纳入侧约束，避免显式枚举和事后过滤全部PI，从而有望降低大规模约束问题中的计算与表示开销。摘要未提供具体实验数据或定量性能比较。

Conclusion: 该工作将PI计算从完整输入空间推广到受约束输入空间，并通过决策图实现约束感知的符号化处理，为逻辑最小化、诊断、可解释形式化方法和人工智能中的可扩展PI分析提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Prime implicants (PIs) are central in computer science, with applications in logic minimization, diagnosis, explainable formal methods and AI. Algorithms for the computation of PIs were first-and-foremost considered on the full input space, not considering the case where the input space might be constrained by context or structural dependencies. To filter out those PIs that do not fulfill the constraints, existing methods rely on an explicit post-processing step after computing all PIs, which leads to scalability issues due to the number of PIs being exponential. We propose end-to-end symbolic algorithms that implicitly represent the set of PIs under side constraints. For this, we extend the prominent Coudert-Madre method based on decision diagrams and implement a modular tool chain that separates decision-diagram construction, PI computation, and filtering.

</details>


### [139] [Extending RTLola with External Data Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06039)
*Bernd Finkbeiner,Jakob Hirschler,Frederik Scheerer,Sebastian Schirmer*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文扩展了流式监控框架 RTLola，使其能够查询外部数据源，从而处理大规模数据，并通过统一接口集成静态数据库和动态 API。实验表明，基于 k-d 树的定制地理空间后端在航空领域规范上优于先进数据库系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流式监控器擅长描述复杂时序属性，但不适合直接处理大规模外部数据。将数据管理任务交给专用系统，可以提升监控能力、可扩展性和数据处理效率。

Method: 在 RTLola 中加入外部数据查询机制，并设计统一接口以连接静态数据库、动态端点和天气 API 等系统。研究并处理响应延迟、返回数据类型检查以及运行时错误管理等问题，同时实现基于 k-d 树的定制地理空间后端。

Result: 该方法能够无缝集成多种外部数据源，并支持流式监控规范访问外部数据。基于航空领域规范的评估显示，定制的 k-d 树地理空间后端性能优于现有先进数据库系统。

Conclusion: 将外部数据查询能力融入流式监控框架，可以有效弥补传统监控器在大规模数据处理方面的不足。统一接口和专用后端提高了系统的实用性与性能，证明了该方法在复杂时序监控场景中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Stream-based monitoring enables the concise specification of complex temporal properties. However, existing stream-based monitors are limited when dealing with large external data sources, a task that is better handled by specialized data management systems. We address these limitations by extending stream-based monitors with the ability to query external data sources. We implement this approach in RTLola and investigate challenges such as handling delayed responses, type checking of returned data, and runtime error management. A unified interface enables the seamless integration of existing systems into our approach, such as static databases or dynamic endpoints, e.g. a weather API. Our evaluation using specifications from the aviation domain also shows that a custom geospatial backend based on k-d trees outperforms state-of-the-art database systems.

</details>


### [140] [Two Ways to See the Future: Combining Prediction and Future-Offset Accesses in RTLola](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06090)
*Jan Baumeister,Bernd Finkbeiner,Eduard Müller,Frederik Scheerer,Julia Tillman*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文扩展了RTLola，使其能够表达和处理依赖未来行为的实时流属性，提出预测操作符与离散未来偏移操作符两种互补机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RTLola面向异步实时系统，但原有语言无法直接描述依赖未来行为的时序属性，限制了其对预测性和延迟可验证需求的表达能力。

Method: 引入预测操作符，根据历史观测外推任意时间戳的未来流值；引入离散未来偏移操作符，通过延迟相关流表达式的求值，在信息可用后访问精确的未来值。论文还对两种扩展进行形式化语义定义，并实现相应机制。

Result: 实验从运行时间和内存消耗两个方面评估了扩展实现，表明两种机制分别支持即时但可能不精确的预测，以及延迟后获得精确结果的未来访问。摘要未提供具体性能数值。

Conclusion: 该工作增强了RTLola对未来依赖属性的表达与运行时监测能力，在即时预测和精确未来值访问之间提供了互补选择，并保持了形式化语义基础。

Abstract: RTLola is a stream-based specification language designed for asynchronous real-time systems. While many temporal specifications naturally refer to future behavior, RTLola currently offers no mechanism to express such future-dependent properties. In this paper, we extend RTLola with two complementary mechanisms to reason about the future. First, we introduce a prediction operator that extrapolates future stream values at arbitrary timestamps based on past observations. Second, we add a discrete future offset operator, which provides access to precise future values by delaying the evaluation of the dependent stream expressions. While the former enables immediate, but possibly imprecise predictions, the latter ensures exact values once the required information becomes available. We formalize both extensions in the RTLola semantics and evaluate their implementation on runtime and memory consumption.

</details>


### [141] [Quantalic lambda-calculus and additive disjunction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06120)
*Renato Neves,Bruna Salgado*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文将加法析取扩展到量化线性λ演算中，建立其等式系统的可靠性，并在特定连续性条件下证明近似完备性，同时展示其在概率与量子计算等模型中的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统程序语义主要关注程序等价性，难以定量分析分支语句及概率行为。本文旨在构建一种能够表达和推理定量程序性质的统一演算框架。

Method: 在量化线性λ演算中引入加法析取，研究扩展后等式系统的可靠性与完备性；在底层量化结构满足连续性条件时，进一步证明近似完备性。随后通过范畴逻辑中的gluing模型、概率模型和量子计算模型进行语义解释，并利用基于Banach空间的概率模型分析随机游走的Cauchy序列。

Result: 证明了扩展演算等式系统的可靠性；在适当连续性假设下，证明其具有近似完备性。研究还构造了多类语义模型，并展示了该演算与Banach空间概率模型结合后可用于推理随机游走序列的定量性质。

Conclusion: 该工作将线性λ演算、量化语义与加法析取结合起来，形成了面向概率、量子计算及泛函分析的定量推理框架，说明程序语义可以从单纯的程序等价性拓展到更灵活的定量分析。

Abstract: Motivated by the need to reason about case statements quantitatively, we extend quantalic linear lambda-calculus with additive disjunction. We show that the resulting equational system is sound. We also show that when certain continuity properties (of the underlying quantale) are adopted, it is additionally (approximately) complete.
  We present several models of the extended calculus, involving for example meta-theoretical properties in categorical logic (gluing), probabilistic, and quantum computation. As a concrete application, we illustrate how a probabilistic model, based on Banach spaces, can be synergistically used with the calculus' equational system to reason about Cauchy sequences of random walks. This highlights the emergent shift from "program semantics as the science of program equivalence" to flexible, quantitative perspectives, involving functional analysis and beyond.

</details>

### [142] [Let it Flow: A Formally Verified Compilation Framework for Asynchronous Dataflow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05451)
*Zhengyao Lin,Yi Cai,Milijana Surbatovich*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: Wavelet是首个形式化验证异步数据流编译器的工作，通过能力类型系统、栅栏机制和Lean证明，兼顾程序确定性与循环流水化，并生成与未验证优化编译器规模相当的数据流图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间数据流架构依靠分布式动态调度、异步通信和高数据局部性提升能效与并行性，但编译过程容易因内存访问冲突和流水化引入非确定性。现有方法缺乏对异步数据流编译器正确性的系统形式化验证。

Method: Wavelet采用分层验证方案：前端使用带栅栏的能力类型系统，对冲突内存访问进行同步并支持跨循环迭代流水化；随后在Lean中形式化并验证两个核心编译阶段，将类型检查器生成的已展开程序转换为数据流图。验证重点包括前向模拟和确定性，并将前端类型系统的健全性语义传递至后续证明，从而实现模拟性与确定性证明的模块化。

Result: 形式化验证证明了核心编译阶段的前向模拟性质和生成程序的确定性。实验表明，Wavelet生成的数据流图规模与面向RipTide空间数据流架构的未验证优化编译器相当，说明形式化保证并未明显牺牲编译结果规模。

Conclusion: Wavelet展示了为异步空间数据流编译器提供端到端关键正确性保证的可行路径。能力类型系统负责约束并发内存访问和支持流水化，Lean形式化则验证编译语义保持与确定性，为构建更可靠的数据流编译基础设施提供了模块化方法。

Abstract: Dataflow architectures have gained renewed interest due to their balance between power efficiency and performance. In (spatial) dataflow architectures, a program is represented as a set of entirely distributed and dynamically scheduled dataflow operators that communicate through asynchronous channels, which greatly improves data locality and parallelism. However, compiling to dataflow architectures remains an error-prone process, in order to maintain determinacy while enabling pipelining. Determinacy means that the result of a dataflow program is deterministic and independent of the schedule of operator execution, and pipelining is an important optimization in spatial dataflow that enables parallelism across loop iterations.
  In this work, we present Wavelet, the first effort to formally verify a compiler for asynchronous dataflow. We use a mix of techniques to achieve this goal. Our frontend uses a novel capability type system with fences to synchronize conflicting memory accesses and enable pipelining. We then verify a Lean formalization of two core passes of our compiler that translates elaborated programs from the type checker to dataflow graphs, proving important properties of forward simulation and determinacy. Notably, our formalization semantically propagates the soundness guarantees of the frontend type system, ensuring modularity between simulation and determinacy proofs. In evaluation, we show that dataflow graphs compiled by Wavelet have comparable sizes to those produced by an unverified optimizing compiler for the RipTide spatial dataflow architecture.

</details>


### [143] [Learning Context-Free Grammars for Grammar-Constrained Decoding via Declarative Agentic Programming with Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05493)
*Kevin Cheang,Geoff Hulette,Rahul Kumar,Felipe R. Monteiro,Federico Mora,Robin Salkeld,Lin Tan,Serdar Tasiran*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: Autogrammar利用文档和执行数据自动学习领域特定语言的上下文无关文法，并通过文法约束解码提升语言模型生成程序的有效性；其效果接近人工维护文法，且显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在低资源、专业化的领域特定语言中容易生成语法错误的程序。文法约束解码能够消除这类错误，但依赖现成的上下文无关文法，而第三方领域特定语言通常缺乏此类资源，因此需要一种自动构建文法的方法。

Method: 研究提出Autogrammar智能体，从语言文档和程序执行数据中自动归纳上下文无关文法。该智能体被形式化为Kripke结构，并由语言模型解析其中的非确定性选择；同时利用线性时序逻辑约束控制智能体行为，以限制搜索过程和执行策略。

Result: 在Amazon CloudWatch Logs Insights、Dynatrace Query Language和Datadog Search Syntax三个领域特定语言上的四个Autogrammar版本均在未见数据上取得接近完美的精确率。加入时序限制后，执行时间缩短3.8倍，且精确率无统计显著下降。执行数据至关重要，而文档信息的作用不明显。使用Autogrammar生成文法进行约束解码后，十项真实任务中有八项的端到端语言模型性能显著提升，达到或超过专业维护文法的效果；其性能也显著优于现有语言模型基线和先进形式化方法生成的文法。

Conclusion: Autogrammar能够有效自动构建高质量的领域特定语言文法，尤其适合缺乏人工语法资源的低资源场景。执行数据是文法学习的核心信息来源，时序约束则能显著提高效率；生成的文法可可靠支持语言模型的语法约束解码。

Abstract: Language models (LMs) are increasingly used to interact with external services via programs written in domain-specific languages (DSLs). Unfortunately, since DSLs are often low-resource and esoteric, LMs frequently produce syntactically invalid programs in these languages. Grammar-constrained decoding can eliminate such failures, but requires syntactic constraints. These are usually in the form of a context-free grammar for the target language, an artifact that is hard to come by for third-party DSLs. In this work, we define an agent, called Autogrammar, that automatically learns context-free grammars from documentation and execution data. Autogrammar is formalized as a Kripke structure whose nondeterministic choices are resolved by a language model, enabling declarative control of agent behavior via linear temporal logic constraints. We evaluate four versions of Autogrammar on three DSLs (i.e., Amazon CloudWatch Logs Insights, Dynatrace Query Language, and Datadog Search Syntax) and find that it generates grammars that achieve near perfect precision on unseen data; that temporal restrictions reduce execution time by 3.8x without incurring statistically-significant loss in precision; that execution data is crucial while documentation is dispensable; and that grammar-constrained decoding using Autogrammar-generated grammars significantly improves end-to-end LM performance on eight out of ten real tasks, matching or exceeding the performance of a professionally-maintained grammar. In comparison, the context-free grammars generated by existing LM baselines and a state-of-the-art formal technique perform significantly worse over the same evaluation.

</details>


### [144] [Noise-aware Verification and Synthesis of Quantum Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05807)
*Stefanie Muroya,Krishnendu Chatterjee,Thomas A. Henzinger*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文研究面向真实噪声量子硬件的噪声感知量子编程，提出噪声感知量子霍尔逻辑及自动化验证与综合方法，并在IBM Qiskit硬件噪声模型上生成噪声最优的量子子程序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子程序语义通常假设理想、无噪声执行，难以反映真实硬件中的设备相关误差。不同硬件的噪声特性会影响程序正确性和最优实现，因此需要将硬件噪声模型纳入程序推理、验证与综合。

Method: 基于量子硬件厂商发布的误差模型，建立硬件相关的量子程序语义；提出噪声感知量子霍尔逻辑，并据此设计特定硬件上的有界验证算法和无循环量子程序自动综合方法。该方法针对奇偶校验、量子态制备和量子态判别等常见子程序进行优化，并使用IBM Qiskit提供的硬件规格开展评估。

Result: 实验表明，不同噪声模型会导致不同的最优量子子程序。综合工具能够针对具体硬件生成噪声代价更低的实现，并发现经典概率分支在某些情况下是实现量子程序最优性的必要机制。

Conclusion: 该工作构建了从噪声语义、形式化验证到自动综合的完整噪声感知量子编程框架，说明量子程序优化应显式考虑硬件噪声；同时，混合使用量子操作与经典概率控制可能比纯量子实现更优。

Abstract: While most research on quantum programming considers an idealized, noise-free semantics for quantum programs, we reason about quantum programs that are executed on real, noisy hardware. We consider the error models published by quantum hardware vendors to give a hardware-dependent semantics to quantum programs. This work presents a comprehensive study of noise-aware quantum programming, ranging from logical foundations to automated verification and synthesis. We develop a noise-aware quantum Hoare logic, and use it to derive algorithmic methods for the bounded verification of quantum programs on specific hardware, and for the automatic synthesis of noise-optimal loop-free quantum programs. In this way, we synthesize hardware-dependent subroutines that commonly occur in quantum algorithms, such as parity checks, quantum state preparation, and quantum state discrimination. We evaluate our method on the hardware specifications provided by the IBM Qiskit toolkit. Besides finding different optimal subroutines for different noise models, our synthesis tool also shows that classical probabilistic branching is needed for optimality in quantum programming.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [145] [Towards a Risk Assessment of Malicious Skill Files in Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05223)
*Rui Yang,Michael Fu,Kla Tantithamthavorn,Chetan Arora,Joey Chua*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统研究企业级自主编码代理中“技能接口”的安全风险，发现伪装成自然语言指令的恶意Shell命令能够高成功率诱导代理执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码代理可动态加载包含指令和脚本的技能文件，并对连接系统拥有较高权限；这种接口可能成为隐藏恶意命令、绕过代理安全机制的新攻击面，因此需要可复现的评估基准和大规模实证分析。

Method: 研究者将471条真实Shell命令通过六个、覆盖四类的LLM改写为外观良性的技能，构建包含2826个技能、覆盖11种MITRE ATT&CK战术的基准。评估流程结合运行分层、证据锚定、拒答否决、确定性声明意图修正，以及三评委LLM-as-a-judge机制，并通过盲人人工金标准验证；随后在两个企业级编码代理的5629次完成运行中开展测量。

Result: Gemini CLI的受攻击率为95.5%—96.1%，Qwen Code为71.6%—74.0%，分别对应原始多数投票到声明意图修正后的估计值，且均符合人工金标准范围。攻击成功率几乎不受生成技能所用模型影响；代理明确识别安全风险的运行仅占1.99%。评估方法与人工标注的一致性较高，Cohen's kappa为0.85。

Conclusion: 技能接口是自主编码代理的重要安全薄弱点，恶意命令可通过良性外观技能实现稳定攻击。企业在部署此类代理前，应对技能内容实施审计、权限隔离、执行控制和持续安全评估，并重点防范自然语言指令与脚本混合带来的风险。

Abstract: Autonomous coding agents are increasingly embedded in enterprise software workflows with delegated authority over connected systems. Central to this architecture is the agent skills interface: folders of instructions and scripts that agents load dynamically to specialize their behavior. This interface also widens the attack surface, letting malicious shell commands hide within natural-language skill files. We make three contributions. First, an adversarial skill-synthesis method using six LLMs across four families to transform 471 real-world shell commands into benign-appearing skills, released as a benchmark of 2,826 skills mapped to 11 MITRE ATT&CK tactics. Second, a reproducible evaluation pipeline coupling run stratification, evidence anchoring, a refusal veto, and a deterministic declared-intent override with a three-judge LLM-as-a-judge panel, validated against a blind human gold standard (Cohen's kappa = 0.85). Third, a large-scale characterization of two enterprise-grade agents across 5,629 completed runs. Gemini CLI is exploited in 95.5-96.1% of runs and Qwen Code in 71.6-74.0% (raw majority vote to declared-intent-corrected estimate, both within the human gold standard), nearly invariant to the generating model. Explicit safety recognition occurs in only 1.99% of runs. Enterprises must assess and mitigate skill-interface risk before adopting coding agents. Our code and dataset are available at https://github.com/awsm-research/AgentJailbreak

</details>


### [146] [Improving Debugging in Verification-Aware Languages Through Automated Fault Localization: A Case Study in Dafny](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05399)
*Álvaro Silva,Isabel Amaral,João Pascoal Faria,Alexandra Mendes*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究面向验证感知语言的自动故障定位，比较基于状态与基于反例的两类方法。实验表明，利用并结构化组织验证器反例信息的方法明显优于基于状态的方法，其中单轨迹排序效果提升最大，多轨迹聚合进一步增强鲁棒性与调试实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dafny等验证感知语言能够通过形式规约进行静态正确性检查，但验证失败反馈通常仅指出违反的条件，难以揭示根本故障。虽然反例可提供执行轨迹，但单条轨迹往往包含大量信息，开发者仍需人工定位真正可疑的程序位置，因此需要自动化且更有效的故障定位方法。

Method: 研究比较两种范式：一是借鉴AutoFix快照思想，通过推断不变量和谓词识别可疑程序状态的基于状态定位；二是逐步增强验证器反例利用方式的基于反例定位，包括原始反例提取、单轨迹结构化排序和多轨迹聚合。实验使用MutDafny基于DafnyBench生成多样化变异体数据集，并采用EXAM分数评估定位效果。

Result: 基于反例的方法整体显著优于基于状态的方法。相较于直接输出原始反例，单轨迹结构化排序带来了最大的性能提升；多轨迹聚合则通过扩大覆盖范围、降低求解器引入的路径偏差，进一步提升了定位的鲁棒性和调试价值。

Conclusion: 在验证感知语言中，实现有效故障定位不仅需要利用验证器生成的反例，还需要对反例进行合理的结构化和多样化处理。单轨迹排序适合提升定位精度，多轨迹聚合则有助于获得更全面、稳定的诊断结果。

Abstract: Verification-aware languages, like Dafny, integrate formal specifications directly into source code to enable static correctness checks. However, when verification fails, the feedback provided is often limited to the specific condition of the error, such as a violated postcondition, rather than the root cause of the fault. While Dafny's counterexample features provide concrete execution traces, these typically expose a single failing path per assertion failure, leaving the developer to manually look through the entire trace to locate the error.
  This paper investigates automated fault localization for verification-aware languages by comparing two paradigms: state-based and counterexample-based localization. Our state-based localization strategy replicates the ``snapshot'' methodology of AutoFix by inferring invariants and predicates to identify suspicious program states. The counterexample-based strategy consists of a family of techniques that progressively enrich the use of verifier output: from raw counterexample extraction, to structured single-trace ranking, and to multi-trace aggregation.
  To validate these methods, we present an evaluation framework using MutDafny to generate a diverse mutant dataset from DafnyBench and measure localization effectiveness using the EXAM score. Our results show that counterexample-based approaches substantially outperform state-based localization in this setting. Structured ranking over a single trace yields the largest improvement over raw counterexample output, while multi-trace aggregation provides additional gains in robustness and debugging utility by increasing coverage and reducing path bias introduced by the solver. These findings demonstrate that effective fault localization in verification-aware languages depends both on using counterexample information, and how that information is structured and diversified.

</details>


### [147] [Reasoning from Traces: Divergence-Guided Agentic Repair of WebAssembly Discrepancies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05521)
*Liyan Huang,Kaicheng Wang,Weihang Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: WasmMend通过差异执行轨迹定位原生版本与Wasm版本首次分歧的函数，再引导LLM分析根因并生成补丁，自动修复跨平台功能差异，修复率达到70.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同一C/C++代码编译为原生二进制和Wasm后，常因库实现差异或编译器缺陷产生运行时不一致。由于根因隐藏在平台运行时与底层实现中，现有基于LLM的修复代理难以有效定位和修复。

Method: 系统分两阶段工作：首先采用差异轨迹分析，比较原生与Wasm执行过程并定位首次发生分歧的函数；随后以该定位结果约束LLM代理的推理范围，分析根因并生成消除差异行为的代码补丁。

Result: 在真实C/C++项目上的实验中，WasmMend修复率为70.0%，高于智能体基线的50.2%以及加入修复时LLM插桩方法的54.5%。

Conclusion: 差异首次发生位置能够显著缩小修复搜索空间并提升LLM的根因分析能力。实验表明，面向分歧的引导式推理是自动修复原生版本与Wasm版本功能差异的有效方法。

Abstract: WebAssembly (Wasm) promises seamless reuse of C/C++ codebases as portable, fast, sandboxed binaries. In practice, however, this promise often falls short: recent studies show that cross-compiling the same C/C++ source to Wasm and native binaries frequently leads to runtime discrepancies, owing to library implementation differences or compiler bugs. Since the root causes lie in the platform-level runtime and are hidden beneath the source code, even state-of-the-art LLM-based repair agents often fail to fix these discrepancies. In this paper, we present WasmMend, the first system to automatically repair Native-Wasm functional discrepancies. WasmMend converts the undirected exploration to a focused reasoning task in two stages: First, a novel differential trace analysis approach localizes the function where Wasm and native executions initially diverge; guided by this localization, LLM agents then reason about the root causes and generate patches that eliminate the divergent behavior. Experiments on real-world C/C++ projects show that WasmMend achieves a fix rate of 70.0%, compared to 50.2% for the agentic baseline and 54.5\% for the approach augmented with repair-time LLM-based instrumentation, demonstrating the value of divergence-guided reasoning for cross-platform repair.

</details>


### [148] [Exploring Dependence, Overreliance, and Addiction Related Behaviors Associated with Large Language Model Use Among Software Engineers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05561)
*Ronnie de Souza Santos,Italo Santos,Matheus de Moraes Leça,Cleyton Magalhaes,Mairieli Wessel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究调查了119名软件从业者在专业开发中使用大语言模型（LLM）时的依赖、过度依赖及成瘾相关行为。结果表明，LLM主要表现为提高效率的功能性依赖，部分参与者存在优先求助LLM而非查阅文档或咨询同事的过度依赖倾向，但成瘾相关行为较少见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM广泛融入代码生成、调试和文档编写等工作流程，有必要理解软件工程师对其形成的依赖程度及潜在风险，从而促进合理使用并避免削弱专业判断。

Method: 研究采用探索性问卷调查，收集119名软件从业者的数据，并结合描述性统计与开放式回答的定性主题分析，考察功能性依赖、过度依赖和成瘾相关行为。

Result: 参与者普遍将LLM视为日常软件工程活动中的常规工具，主要依赖原因是生产力和效率提升。部分回答显示，参与者会优先使用LLM而不是查阅文档或咨询同事，但通常仍会验证生成结果。成瘾相关表现较少，主要涉及难以控制使用频率或对技术产生情感依附，并未普遍出现明显的失控或功能受损。

Conclusion: LLM正在成为专业软件开发中的习惯性组成部分。当前使用总体以功能性依赖为主，但应通过信任校准、保持专业判断和持续验证输出等措施，推动适度依赖，降低过度依赖风险。

Abstract: The widespread adoption of Large Language Models (LLMs) has changed how software engineers perform everyday development activities. While these systems provide substantial support for tasks such as code generation, debugging, and documentation, their increasing integration into professional workflows has also raised questions regarding developers' reliance on these tools and the emergence of dependence, overreliance, and addiction-related behaviors. This study investigates how software engineers experience the use of LLMs during professional software development, with attention to behavioral patterns associated with dependence, overreliance, and addiction-related behaviors. An exploratory survey was conducted with 119 software practitioners. The data were analyzed using descriptive statistics and qualitative thematic analysis of participants' open-ended responses. Participants primarily described functional dependence, with LLMs becoming integrated into routine software engineering activities because of the productivity and efficiency they provide. Responses also suggested patterns consistent with overreliance, particularly through prioritizing LLMs over documentation or peer consultation while continuing to verify generated outputs. Reports associated with addiction-related behaviors were less common and primarily reflected difficulty moderating use or emotional attachment to the technology rather than impaired control. The findings suggest that LLMs are becoming a habitual component of professional software engineering practice. While most reported use appears functional, the results indicate the importance of promoting appropriate reliance by supporting trust calibration, professional judgment, and verification throughout software development.

</details>


### [149] [JTA: Joint Testability Architecture for Scenario-Based Validation of Safety-Critical Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05594)
*Wenyao Xue,Jiandi Wang,Yichen Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出联合可测试性架构（JTA），将测试场景、测试系统与被测系统作为统一分析对象，从可控性、可观测性和可隔离性三个维度评估安全关键软件的场景验证能力，并通过架构化方法识别验证盲点、设计改进措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可测试性研究主要关注被测软件或系统本身，难以解释场景构造、测试执行、证据整理与异常归因之间的协同关系。安全关键软件需要在受控条件下复现关键场景，将执行证据组织为可判定结果，并把异常现象归因到可操作的原因，因此需要一种覆盖场景、测试系统和被测系统的整体架构。

Method: JTA将三类对象联合建模，围绕可控性、可观测性和可隔离性构建分析框架，并组织为三个域、三个桥接关系以及“分析—设计—评估—改进”循环。该方法引入场景契约、联合能力评估、验证盲点识别和面向桥接关系的设计行动，将能力缺口映射到控制点增强、证据组织优化和归因边界明确等具体改进。

Result: 在ArduPilot失效保护验证示例中，链路丢失场景的验证能力相对成熟；相比之下，状态估计异常场景更难验证，主要原因是证据对齐能力不足、异常归因语义不够明确。该示例表明，JTA能够揭示传统测试视角下容易被忽略的联合验证能力缺口。

Conclusion: JTA为安全关键软件的场景化验证提供了架构基础，可统一支持验证能力建模、设计、评估和迭代改进。它并不替代现有测试或安全分析技术，而是补充其在跨场景、测试系统与被测系统协同推理方面的不足。

Abstract: Validation adequacy in safety-critical software depends on more than the system under test. Critical scenarios must be constructed under controlled conditions, execution evidence must be aligned into verdict-ready form, and abnormal outcomes must be attributable to actionable causes. Existing testability research remains largely artifact-centric and offers little architectural support for reasoning about the combined capability of the scenario, the test system, and the system under test. Joint Testability Architecture (JTA) addresses this gap by treating those three elements as a single object of analysis and design. It characterizes validation capability along three dimensions--controllability, observability, and isolability--and organizes them through three domains, three bridges, and an analysis-design-evaluation-refinement loop. JTA also introduces scenario contracts, joint capability assessment, validation blind-spot identification, and bridge-oriented design actions that map capability gaps to concrete improvements in control points, evidence organization, and attribution boundaries. An illustrative analysis of ArduPilot failsafe validation shows that link-loss scenarios are comparatively mature, whereas state-estimation anomaly scenarios remain harder to validate because evidence alignment and attribution semantics are weaker. JTA is not a replacement for existing testing or safety-analysis techniques; it provides an architectural basis for modeling, designing, and assessing scenario-based validation in safety-critical software.

</details>


### [150] [Towards Competence-Based Management for Open Source Software Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05599)
*Sabahat Younas,Márcia Moraes,Fabio Santos*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究通过分析贡献者提交代码的度量指标，评估其能力水平，并提出一个能够预测任务所需能力等级的能力模型，以支持开源项目识别和培养核心贡献者替代人选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源项目核心贡献者退出会威胁项目和社区的持续运行。现有方法虽然能够提供贡献者能力的初步线索，但难以深入衡量能力等级，导致项目维护者难以准确判断候选人的实际技术能力，且招聘、面试和培训成本较高。

Method: 研究收集贡献者在源代码贡献中的代码度量指标，并据此评估贡献者能力；同时构建能力模型，用于预测完成特定任务所需的能力水平。研究还提供了包含代码、数据和文档的复现实验包。

Result: 研究提出了一种基于代码度量的贡献者能力评估方法，以及一个可预测任务能力需求等级的模型。该方法旨在比既有研究更细致地刻画贡献者能力，并帮助识别适合培养为核心贡献者的候选人。摘要未报告具体数据集规模、模型性能或统计显著性结果。

Conclusion: 通过更准确地评估贡献者能力，开源项目可以识别潜在接班人并制定针对性培训方案，从而缓解核心贡献者流失带来的维护风险。该研究的实际有效性仍需结合完整实验结果和跨项目复现进行验证。

Abstract: Contributors to Open Source Software (OSS) projects are vital to maintaining the health of both communities and projects. However, the number of projects experiencing core contributors' disengagement has increased to the point that it risks the projects' survival. Finding new contributors to replace the workforce is challenging and time-intensive due to several factors, including a lack of precise knowledge about potential candidates' competences, which may need to be confirmed through interviews and exams. Previous studies provided indications of the contributor's competences, although they lack depth in understanding competence levels, which can result in poor knowledge about the contributor's capabilities. To address this gap, we assess contributors' competence by collecting code metrics related to source code from contributions. We also propose a competence model able to predict the competence level required to solve tasks. By properly assessing contributors' competences, we can identify and train candidates to replace core contributors. Our replication package, including code, data, and documentation, is available at https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.21605122

</details>


### [151] [Keeping Models and Code in Sync: Roundtrip Engineering for Tactical Domain-Driven Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05612)
*Weixing Zhang,Mario Herb,Wai Chung Dorothy Cheng,Michael Wagner,Bowen Jiang,Tianhai Liu,Anne Koziolek*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出JDomInO，一套面向战术领域驱动设计（DDD）的双向同步工具链，通过共享元模型连接Java代码库与领域模型，以减缓两者在演化过程中的语义漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DDD中的共享词汇和领域模型只有在模型与代码保持一致时才具有持续价值。然而实际开发中，代码变更可能快于模型更新，或模型修订无法同步到代码库，导致两者逐渐偏离。因此需要一种能够在模型与代码之间进行可靠双向转换和同步的机制。

Method: JDomInO基于统一共享元模型实现两条路径：正向路径从领域模型确定性地生成Java代码结构；反向路径从现有Java代码重建领域模型。研究以包含元模型全部12类构建块的酒店管理场景验证正向生成能力，并通过单元测试检验反向映射逻辑。此外，论文讨论将结构化领域模型作为AI代码助手的精确上下文层，以提供聚合边界和DDD语义信息。

Result: 正向路径已在酒店管理场景中完成全面验证，覆盖元模型中的全部12类构建块；反向路径的映射逻辑已通过单元测试，但端到端验证仍在进行中。因此，当前证据主要支持正向生成的有效性，尚不足以完整证明双向同步在真实项目中的可靠性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: JDomInO为保持DDD领域模型与Java代码一致提供了可行的双向同步基础，并有望降低模型—代码漂移。其进一步价值在于为AI代码助手提供显式、结构化的领域上下文。不过，反向路径仍需完成端到端测试，并通过更大规模、多样化项目验证同步准确性、冲突处理能力及长期维护成本。

Abstract: Domain-Driven Design gives teams a shared vocabulary for complex business logic, but that vocabulary only stays useful as long as the model and the code agree with each other. In practice, they drift apart: code changes outpace the model, or model revisions never make it into the codebase. This paper presents JDomInO, a bidirectional synchronization toolchain for tactical DDD that keeps a Java codebase and its domain model connected through a shared metamodel, with the goal of keeping the two in sync as the system evolves. JDomInO generates Java code structure deterministically from a domain model (forward path) and reconstructs a domain model from existing Java code (reverse path). The forward path has been fully validated on a Hotel Management scenario covering all 12 building block types in the metamodel; the reverse path's mapping logic has passed unit testing, with end-to-end validation underway. We also outline how the structured domain model produced by JDomInO could serve as a precision context layer for AI code assistants, helping them respect aggregate boundaries and DDD semantics that raw source code alone does not convey.

</details>


### [152] [Agent-Based Test Assertion Generation via Diverse Perspective Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05822)
*Dong Wang,Qiaoyu Han,Lin Yang,Jianyi Zhou,Guangtai Liang,Junjie Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: AssertMate是一种基于多智能体协作的LLM断言生成框架，通过实际值构造、多视角期望值预测和LLM评审选择，提高自动生成测试断言的编译成功率、通过率与缺陷检测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有断言生成方法存在准确率有限、依赖过度采样以及单次提示导致结果受模型随机性影响等问题，因此需要一种更稳定、可靠且能综合多种推理视角的生成方法。

Method: AssertMate包含三个核心组件：首先利用静态分析和类型感知启发式方法定位断言目标并构造实际值；其次通过代码生成、检索增强生成和思维链推理等智能体，从多个视角预测期望值；最后利用LLM-as-a-Judge协作机制评估并选择最合适的断言。

Result: 在Defects4J基准上的实验表明，AssertMate在编译成功率、测试通过率和缺陷检测能力方面显著优于现有先进方法。与EvoSuite集成后，其突变覆盖率和被杀死的突变体数量也更高。消融实验显示，三个组件均能带来显著且互补的性能提升。

Conclusion: 聚合代码生成、检索增强、思维链推理和LLM评审等多种视角，能够有效提升LLM断言生成的准确性、稳定性和实用性。AssertMate验证了基于智能体协作的自动断言生成框架具有较大应用潜力。

Abstract: Test assertions are critical elements of unit tests, serving as checkpoints to validate expected behavior and ensure software correctness. Numerous techniques have been proposed to automate assertion generation, with recent progress notably driven by large language models (LLMs). Despite the promise, existing approaches such as ChatAssert suffer from modest accuracy, heavy reliance on oversampling, and vulnerability to model randomness due to one-shot prompting. To address these limitations, we propose AssertMate, a novel agent-based assertion generation framework that enhances the quality and reliability of LLM-generated assertions through three key components: (1) actual value construction that identifies assertion targets via static analysis and type-aware heuristics; (2) multi-perspective expected value prediction using code generation, retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), and chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning agents; and (3) an LLM-as-a-Judge collaboration mechanism to select the most appropriate assertion. Evaluation on the Defects4J benchmark demonstrates that AssertMate significantly outperforms state-of-the-art techniques in compilation success and pass rates, along with substantially higher bug detection capabilities. Integration with EvoSuite further validates AssertMate's practicality, yielding superior mutation coverage and kill counts. Ablation studies reveal that each of the three components makes a significant and complementary contribution to the overall performance. This work affirms the great potential of aggregating diverse perspectives to enhance the effectiveness of LLM-based assertion generation.

</details>


### [153] [CodeGrep: An RL-Trained Retrieval Agent for LLM Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05886)
*Wuya Chen,Yihao yang,Yang Cao,Yue Lin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: CodeGrep 是一个面向代码库检索的 14B 检索智能体，通过多轮并行调用 grep、glob 和文件读取工具，为下游编码智能体提供候选文件。在 SWE-Bench Verified 上，它在保持修复率的同时减少了交互轮数和 token 消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代 LLM 编码智能体在修复代码前往往耗费大量预算进行仓库探索，而非实际修改代码。论文旨在通过专门的检索智能体降低文件定位成本，并研究检索质量与下游修复效率之间的关系。

Method: 使用 67K 条开源智能体轨迹，通过 CATM 挖掘训练监督；采用 GRPO 对 14B CodeGrep 进行端到端训练，使其能够多轮并行执行 grep、glob 和 read 操作，并将候选文件交给冻结的下游编码智能体。研究还构建了支持多轮智能体强化学习的 Git-worktree 环境，并比较 BM25、Jina 与 CodeGrep。效率信号被施加在 advantage 层，而非 reward 层，以降低 KL 漂移。

Result: 在全部 500 个 SWE-Bench Verified 实例上，CodeGrep 的修复率为 27.0%，高于无检索基线的 25.8%；在成功修复的实例中，平均交互轮数减少 15%，token 消耗减少 19%。检索器效用存在精度阈值：BM25 精度为 0.375 时会损害性能，Jina 精度为 0.445 时基本中性，而 CodeGrep 精度为 0.677 时开始显著降低 rollout 成本。

Conclusion: 专用代码检索智能体能够有效减少编码智能体的仓库探索开销，并在不牺牲修复率的情况下提升整体效率。研究表明，检索质量必须超过一定精度阈值才会产生下游收益；将效率信号施加于 advantage 层有助于稳定训练并改善效率迁移。

Abstract: Modern LLM coding agents such as Claude Code and OpenHands share a common inefficiency: they spend much of their token budget finding the file to patch, rather than patching it. On SWE-Bench Verified, a 30B OpenHands agent averages 23 rounds and 631K tokens per resolved issue, with many calls spent on grep, glob, and view_file during repository exploration. We introduce CodeGrep, a 14B retrieval agent trained end-to-end with GRPO to issue multi-turn parallel grep, glob, and read tool calls and return candidate files to a frozen downstream coding agent.
  On all 500 SWE-Bench Verified instances, CodeGrep preserves resolve rate while substantially improving efficiency: 27.0% versus 25.8% for the no-retrieval baseline, with 15% fewer rounds and 19% fewer tokens on resolved instances. Across retrievers, downstream utility follows a precision threshold: BM25 with precision 0.375 degrades the agent, Jina with precision 0.445 is neutral, and CodeGrep with precision 0.677 crosses the threshold at which retrieval begins to reduce rollout cost. To enable this study, we mine supervision from 67K open-source agent trajectories using CATM and build a Git-worktree environment for multi-turn agent RL. In our setting, applying the efficiency signal at the advantage layer rather than the reward layer reduces KL drift and translates cleanly into downstream efficiency. We will release the model, training pipeline, RL environment, and evaluation harnesses.

</details>


### [154] [Mapping the Emerging Curriculum for AI-Assisted Software Engineering via Syllabus Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05898)
*Francis Geng,Anshul Shah,Mia Chen,Paul Denny,Juho Leinonen,Bill Griswold,Gerald Soosai Raj,Leo Porter*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过分析23份公开的高年级、学分制课程教学大纲及相关材料，系统刻画高校面向生成式人工智能辅助软件开发的课程设置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式人工智能正在改变专业软件开发流程，高校需要相应课程培养学生的人工智能辅助开发能力。现有课程尚处于形成阶段，对其教学目标、内容和评价方式缺乏系统性认识，因此有必要通过课程材料分析总结该领域的课程实践，为未来课程设计提供依据。

Method: 研究者筛选了23份符合标准的公开课程大纲和课程材料，要求课程为高年级、学分制课程，并明确涉及软件工程中的生成式人工智能。研究采用迭代式定性编码，分析课程的学习目标、考核方式、教学主题以及所记录的人工智能工具。

Result: 研究发现，不同课程在学习目标、评估方式、教学主题和工具使用方面既存在共同点，也存在明显差异。这些共性与差异反映了生成式人工智能软件工程课程正在形成的基本框架及其多样化实践。

Conclusion: 该研究为生成式人工智能辅助软件开发课程提供了初步的实证画像，可帮助研究者和教育者理解当前课程如何定义这一新兴领域，并为后续课程开发、教学设计和课程评估提供参考。

Abstract: As Generative AI coding tools reshape professional software development, universities have begun designing courses to prepare students for AI-assisted development workflows. By analyzing the syllabi of these courses, we can gather empirical evidence about these courses, reveal how this emerging curricular area is being defined, and gain guidance for future curriculum design. We analyzed 23 publicly available syllabi and course materials of upper-division, credit-bearing courses that meet specific criteria, including explicitly addressing Generative AI in software engineering. Through iterative qualitative coding, we characterized courses' learning objectives, assessments, topics, and documented AI tools. Our analysis reveals commonalities and differences among these courses that allow researchers and educators to study and develop future courses.

</details>


### [155] [Escaping the Self-Repair Trap: Improving Test Oracle Generation via Dual-Context Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05917)
*Kefan Li,Hongyue Yu,Yuan Yuan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文指出，基于执行成功率的迭代自修复可能陷入“自修复陷阱”：生成的断言越来越容易满足，却越来越难发现程序缺陷。为此提出非迭代框架DCAware，通过结构化静态上下文与选择性动态状态检索提升回归预言机质量，在保持高执行成功率的同时增强缺陷揭示能力，并降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有回归预言机生成方法常将执行成功作为优化目标，但该指标只是代理目标，可能与真正的缺陷揭示效果不一致。反复修复会使模型偏向生成宽松、易满足但检测能力较弱的断言，因此需要以高质量上下文替代复杂的多轮反馈优化。

Method: DCAware采用非迭代式生成流程，优先构建高信噪比的上下文基础。方法融合结构化静态信息与经过选择的动态程序状态，为大语言模型提供更精准、稳健的生成依据，从而避免执行反馈驱动的重复修复过程。

Result: 基于执行测试和变异测试的实验表明，DCAware在保持较高执行成功率的同时，持续提升了预言机的缺陷揭示能力，并优于已有方法；此外，其计算成本显著低于依赖多轮迭代修复的方法。

Conclusion: 在研究的回归预言机生成场景中，提升上下文质量比增加迭代修复复杂度更有效。执行成功率不应被视为唯一优化目标，评估和优化过程还应直接关注预言机的缺陷揭示能力。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown strong potential for regression-oracle completion, where a test prefix is given and the current program version is treated as expected behavior. Recent approaches increasingly rely on iterative self-repair and execution feedback, but optimizing execution success does not necessarily yield strong fault-revealing oracles. This objective, widely adopted in repair-based methods, serves only as a proxy and may be misaligned with the true goal of oracle generation. Such misalignment biases the repair process, giving rise to a feedback-driven degeneration that we term the Self-Repair Trap, where iterative repair progressively drives models toward assertions that are easier to satisfy but less effective at detecting faults. To address this issue, we propose DCAware, a computationally efficient, non-iterative framework that prioritizes high signal-to-noise contextual grounding over multi-round repair. DCAware integrates structured static context with selectively retrieved dynamic states, enabling precise and robust oracle generation without iterative feedback loops. Extensive experiments based on execution and mutation testing show that DCAware consistently improves fault-revealing effectiveness while maintaining high execution success, outperforming prior methods with substantially lower computational cost. Our results suggest that improving contextual quality is more effective than adding iterative repair complexity in the studied regression-oracle setting.

</details>


### [156] [Expertise-Based Developer Assignment for Long-Term Software Components in Open-Source Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05919)
*Faheem Ullah,Babar Shah,William Shanks,Ayesha Mohsin,Abrar Ullah*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Git提交历史的开发者专长建模方法，并通过Web应用将开发者自动分配至新项目中的长期、专长相关任务。实验表明，系统在47名候选开发者中，有72.4%的任务将目标开发者排在前10名以内；缓存机制使最差情况下的速度提升9.86倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开发者任务分配研究多针对特定类型的短期工作，对新项目中长期组件的专长匹配关注不足。错误分配会导致任务完成时间延长和代码可维护性下降，因此需要一种能够利用历史开发行为进行自动化匹配的方法。

Method: 构建一个Web应用，分析开发者过去的Git提交记录以建立专长模型，并根据新项目任务的专长需求对开发者进行排序和分配。系统同时比较不同后端模型的运行速度，并采用服务器端数据缓存优化性能。

Result: 系统性能受后端模型影响明显。服务器端缓存使最差情况下的响应速度提升9.86倍。在47名候选开发者中，72.4%的任务能够将实际目标开发者列入排名前10。

Conclusion: 基于Git提交历史的开发者专长建模能够支持新项目中的自动任务分配，并具备一定的实际有效性。该方法在排序准确率和系统性能方面均显示出潜力，但仍需进一步验证模型在更多项目、任务类型和开发者群体中的泛化能力。

Abstract: Open-source software development through GitHub has enabled countless software projects to be developed by developers from across the world. Assigning the right task to the right developer enables teams to work efficiently. Assigning a task which is not representative of a developer's expertise results in slower completion time and less maintainable code. Whilst much work has been done to automate this assignment for specific jobs, there is little work addressing the assignment of developers to expertise-specific long-term components of a new project. This paper produces a web application that models developers' expertise based on their previous Git commits and automatically assigns them to an optimal task within a new project. Testing showed that the system's speed varies depending on the back-end model used but server-side data caching improved worst-case speeds by a factor of 9.86. 72.4% of tasks had their target developer listed in the top 10, out of a possible 47.

</details>


### [157] [Sensor-Level Fault Diagnosis for Automotive Software Validation Using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05921)
*Mohammad Abboush,Hamza Ouarrad,Andreas Rausch*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究评估了开源指令微调大语言模型在汽车硬件在环验证中进行故障检测与诊断的可行性。模型基于传感器行为的文本化描述，在六类注入故障上实现最高81.6%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HIL验证会产生大量多变量传感器记录，人工审查难以扩展；传统阈值工具只能发现偏差，数据驱动分类器又依赖大规模标注数据且缺乏可解释性，难以满足ISO 26262的可追溯性要求。

Method: 首先在dSPACE实时平台上自动检查功能安全要求，筛选出违反要求的记录；随后对信号滑动窗口提取统计、关系和上下文特征，组织为固定格式提示词，并使用4 bit低秩适配的开源指令微调LLM输出故障位置及文字解释。研究比较了参数规模为2B至8B的四类模型，并在汽油机案例和六类注入故障上进行测试。

Result: 最小模型达到81.6%的准确率，与最大模型相当；另一款规模相近的模型未能收敛。结果表明，诊断能力主要取决于任务适配后的收敛情况，而非参数规模。完整的适配与评估流程可在单个消费级加速器上完成。

Conclusion: 经任务适配的开源LLM有望成为数据高效且具可解释性的汽车软件故障诊断工具，适合嵌入现有验证流程。模型规模并非性能的决定因素，但收敛稳定性、输出可靠性和面向ISO 26262的解释可追溯性仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: The pre-series validation of automotive software on hardware-in-the-loop (HIL) platforms produces large volumes of multivariate sensor recordings whose assessment against functional safety requirements exceeds what manual review can sustain at campaign scale. Threshold-based tooling reports that a deviation has occurred but neither identifies its nature nor locates its source, while data-driven classifiers, although accurate, rely on large labelled datasets and return opaque decisions that sit uneasily with the traceability demanded by ISO 26262. This study examines whether open-source instruction-tuned large language models (LLMs), given a textual description of sensor behaviour, can serve as data-efficient and interpretable engines for fault detection and diagnosis inside the validation loop. A two-phase framework is proposed: automated requirement checking on a dSPACE real-time platform first isolates the recordings that violate a safety requirement, and only these are inspected, with sliding windows of the signals reduced to statistical, relational, and contextual descriptors, embedded in a fixed prompt, and mapped by a 4-bit low-rank-adapted LLM to a fault location accompanied by a written justification. Four model families ranging from two to eight billion parameters were adapted and tested on a gasoline-engine case study spanning six injected fault classes. The smallest model matched the largest at 81.6\% accuracy, whereas a comparably sized model failed to converge, indicating that diagnostic competence under task-specific adaptation follows convergence rather than parameter count, with the entire adapt-and-evaluate cycle fitting on a single commodity accelerator.

</details>


### [158] [AgentExecutor: Partial Code Execution via Agentic Context Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05959)
*Junkai Chen,Chengran Yang,Xing Hu,Zhenhao Li,Xin Xia,David Lo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出AgentExecutor，一个用于不完整代码片段执行的多智能体框架。该方法通过环境准备、迭代式动态探索和基于程序合成的前缀演化，提高代码片段的可执行性、覆盖率与执行效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任意代码片段通常缺少运行上下文、依赖项或资源文件，导致难以直接执行。现有方法如LExecutor和Treefix虽利用语言模型补全缺失信息，但其可执行操作范围有限、反馈利用不足，且优化策略较为固定，因此在执行质量、效率和成本方面仍存在局限。

Method: AgentExecutor采用三阶段设计：第一，准备执行环境，补齐配置、依赖和资源等运行条件；第二，利用具备思考、行动和反馈迭代能力的语言模型智能体进行动态探索与逐步修复，可执行创建资源文件、调整环境配置等更丰富操作；第三，采用程序合成进行代码前缀演化，并结合覆盖率引导的上下文裁剪，自适应地优化输入上下文与执行路径。

Result: 在Stack Overflow代码片段和开源项目代码两个数据集上，AgentExecutor的代码覆盖率最高分别达到94%和90%。相较于现有最优方法Treefix，其覆盖率分别提升19.9%和13.8%；同时，执行时间最高降低80.3%，成本最高降低56.6%。

Conclusion: AgentExecutor表明，多智能体驱动的迭代探索与自适应优化能够有效解决部分代码执行中的上下文和依赖缺失问题。其关键贡献在于扩展可操作空间、充分利用执行反馈，并以覆盖率和程序合成为导向持续优化，从而同时改善执行成功质量、时间效率和调用成本。

Abstract: Executing code snippets is essential for dynamic program analysis, but it remains challenging to execute an arbitrary code snippet due to issues like missing context and incomplete dependencies. Existing approaches to partial code execution, such as LExecutor and Treefix, leverage the power of language models to infer missing information and enable execution. However, they suffer from (i) limited action spaces and feedback, and (ii) rigid optimization strategies, which restrict their effectiveness and efficiency. In this paper, we propose AgentExecutor, a novel multi-agent framework for partial code execution. Our approach introduces a three-phase design: execution environment preparation, dynamic exploration with iterative refinement, and prefix evolution via program synthesis. Supported by the power of LLM agents who can think, act, and get feedback iteratively, AgentExecutor is able to autonomously explore a richer action space, enabling diverse operations such as creating resource files and resolving environment configuration. Furthermore, it adopts adaptive optimization strategies, including coverage-guided context pruning and prefix evolution via program synthesis, to systematically improve the execution quality of partial code. We evaluate AgentExecutor on two widely used datasets comprising Stack Overflow snippets and open-source project code. The results show that AgentExecutor achieves up to 94% and 90% code coverage, outperforming the state-of-the-art approach Treefix by 19.9% and 13.8%, respectively. In addition, AgentExecutor significantly reduces execution time (by up to 80.3%) and cost (by up to 56.6%). These findings demonstrate that AgentExecutor provides an effective and efficient solution for partial code execution.

</details>


### [159] [LangChoiceBench: Measuring and Explaining Programming-Language Choice in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06041)
*Lukas Twist,Twm Stone,Helen Yannakoudakis,Jie M. Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出LangChoiceBench，用于系统评估大语言模型在项目级代码生成中的Python偏好、推荐与实现一致性及语言多样性。对25个模型的评测表明，模型普遍过度选择Python，且推荐与实际实现的一致性较低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究已发现大语言模型在项目级代码生成中偏好Python，但缺乏可跨模型、系统化的测量方法，尤其无法充分评估语言选择是否符合项目需求及模型推荐与实现之间的一致性。

Method: 构建LangChoiceBench，涵盖7个软件领域的28个项目，这些项目通常不适合默认使用Python。研究评估25个不同类型的大语言模型，并分析9,826条推理轨迹，以考察语言选择偏好、语言多样性、推荐—实现一致性及选择依据。

Result: 模型仍显著过度选择Python；推荐与最终实现之间的一致性较低。较小的开放权重模型通常表现出更强的Python偏好和更低的语言多样性。推理轨迹显示，多数Python选择是自动产生的，或主要受易用性驱动，而非基于项目需求的明确分析。部分案例出现“虚幻证据”现象，即模型捏造上下文依据来支持Python选择；另有案例中生成的代码与其自身推理中选择的语言不一致。

Conclusion: LangChoiceBench揭示了大语言模型在项目级编程中的系统性语言选择偏差。模型不仅倾向于机械选择Python，还可能缺乏需求导向的语言决策能力，并产生虚构依据或推理—实现不一致问题。未来模型评测与训练应加强项目约束理解、语言多样性和决策可验证性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have been shown to exhibit strong Python preferences when generating project-level code, but there is currently no systematic way to measure this behaviour across new models. To bridge this gap, we introduce LangChoiceBench, a project-level code-generation benchmark for measuring Python preference, recommendation-implementation consistency, and language diversity. LangChoiceBench covers 28 projects across seven software areas where Python is often a poor default. We evaluate 25 diverse LLMs and find that Python remains heavily over-selected, recommendation-implementation consistency is low, and smaller open-weight models generally show stronger Python preference and lower language diversity. We further analyse 9,826 reasoning traces and find that most Python choices are automatic or driven primarily by ease, rather than explicit consideration of project requirements. In a smaller but important set of cases, models fabricate contextual support for choosing Python - a failure mode we call phantom evidence - or produce code that contradicts the language selected in their own reasoning.

</details>


### [160] [In Terms of Explainability: Refining Requirements for Self-Explainable Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06049)
*Arno Leue,Akhila Bairy,Maike Schwammberger*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对自主软件密集型系统缺乏统一可解释性定义与开发指南的问题，整合既有研究提出统一定义、结构化需求与分类体系，并强调解释质量及正确性，为标准制定、认证和审计提供基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主系统日益复杂并承担更高责任，透明性和可解释性成为关键属性。然而现有研究缺少普适的定义、开发指导和评价依据，相关标准化需求已受到欧盟人工智能法案及IEEE 7001-2021透明性标准的关注。

Method: 分析并整合现有可解释性定义，构建统一术语体系；进一步提出构建自解释系统所需的结构化可解释性要求，并通过分析形成的分类体系，将解释质量与解释正确性纳入统一定义。

Result: 形成了面向自主及软件密集型系统的统一可解释性定义和结构化需求框架，明确提出解释不仅应具备可理解性，还应具有良好质量和正确性。该分类体系可支持可解释系统的设计、规范化描述与评价。

Conclusion: 研究为自解释系统的正式标准制定提供了基础，并为后续的系统认证、合规审计和一致性评价提供了统一分类与术语框架。

Abstract: Autonomous and software-intensive systems have been growing in occurrence, complexity, and assumed responsibility. Due to the high complexity of these systems, properties like transparency and explainability must be a focus of investigation. To date, no universally applicable definition and guide for the development of (self-)explainable systems exists. A need for explainability standards has already been recognized in the EU AI Act and the IEEE Transparency Standard 7001-2021. To address this need, we propose unified definitions in explainability based on an analysis and combination of existing definitions. Additionally, we present structured explainability requirements that are necessary to build (self-)explainable systems. By analysing the resulting taxonomy, we propose the incorporation of explanation goodness and thus correctness of explanations into the unified definitions. With our approach, we support the development of formal standards for (self-)explainable systems. Establishing such a uniform taxonomy also is a beneficial step towards certifying or auditing explainable systems.

</details>


### [161] [DCAS: Decoupling CLI Agent Scaffolding to Internalize Planning across Scaffolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06113)
*Kishanthan Thangarajah,Boyuan Chen,Ahmed E. Hassan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究CLI软件工程智能体在训练脚手架与部署脚手架之间的性能迁移差距，指出该差距主要由微调过程学习了特定脚手架的规划结构所致，并提出DCAS以解耦脚手架与后端模型。实验表明，将规划能力从固定脚手架迁移至模型本身，可显著提升跨脚手架泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放生态中的轨迹数据几乎都在OpenHands环境下采集，导致模型在OpenHands上表现良好，但换用其他CLI脚手架后性能明显下降。由于未训练基础模型不存在同样的性能分化，作者推断问题源于微调所吸收的脚手架特定行为，尤其是规划结构，而非模型本身的通用能力不足。

Method: 作者提出Decoupling CLI Agent Scaffolding（DCAS），通过后端替换式拦截层在不修改CLI脚手架的情况下转发API流量，使任意脚手架能够接入任意后端模型，并支持跨脚手架评测及规划感知轨迹采集。研究区分显式规划（执行前生成的独立计划）与隐式规划（贯穿智能体循环、由脚手架约定塑造的执行结构），并通过控制性的计划来源干预和跨脚手架微调实验验证二者作用。

Result: 计划来源干预显示，规划质量是影响性能的高杠杆因素，其带来的增益超过了跨脚手架部署造成的性能下降。仅使用单一脚手架下少量DCAS规划感知轨迹进行微调，模型便能在多个非训练脚手架上取得稳定收益。此外，实验表明显式规划与隐式规划在训练数据中具有经验上的可分离性。

Conclusion: CLI智能体的跨脚手架性能差距并非单纯的模型能力问题，而是微调阶段对特定脚手架规划规范的过度依赖。DCAS为解耦脚手架和模型提供了实用工具；将规划从固定脚手架机制转化为模型可学习、可迁移的能力，是提升开放CLI智能体泛化性的有效路径。

Abstract: CLI-based software-engineering agents have matured rapidly, yet the open ecosystem has converged on a single training environment: trajectory datasets used to fine-tune open models are collected almost exclusively under OpenHands. Models fine-tuned on this data score well under OpenHands but degrade substantially when deployed under any non-training scaffold. Untrained base models do not show this divergence, indicating the gap is fine-tuning-induced and tied to the conventions of the training scaffold. We argue that a load-bearing scaffold-specific behavior is planning structure, in two senses this paper distinguishes: explicit planning, a pre-execution plan produced as a first-class artifact, and implicit planning, the structural conventions that shape execution throughout the agent loop. Under this hypothesis, closing the gap requires moving planning from a fixed scaffold artifact to a learned model capability. We introduce Decoupling CLI Agent Scaffolding (DCAS), a backend-substitution interception layer that routes API traffic between any CLI scaffold and any backend model without modifying the scaffold, enabling cross-scaffold evaluation and planning-aware trajectory collection. Using DCAS, a controlled plan-source intervention confirms planning quality is a high-leverage component, with gains exceeding the cross-scaffold drops we observe. A model fine-tuned on a small set of DCAS-collected planning-aware trajectories under a single scaffold gains consistently across non-training scaffolds, and the two senses of planning are empirically separable in training data.

</details>


### [162] [Learning Globally Reusable Skills for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06153)
*Chen Yang,Jiashuo Tian,Ziqi Wang,Xinyin Liu,Meiru Ye,Junjie Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: GSE是一种面向大语言模型智能体的全局化技能演化框架，通过建模技能关系、聚类 consolidating 技能以及回放验证，提升技能兼容性、泛化能力并减少过拟合。在软件工程任务中，该方法在精确率、召回率和F1值上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能演化方法通常进行局部、序列式更新，忽略技能之间的相互关系，容易产生过拟合的技能更新，导致跨任务泛化能力不足，并可能引发行为退化。

Method: GSE维护技能关系图（SRG），显式建模并协同演化不同技能之间的关系，以保持技能库的一致性；同时采用基于聚类的技能整合，从局部更新中抽象可复用能力，并通过回放驱动的验证机制检测过拟合和行为回归。

Result: 在缺陷揭示测试生成和误报缺陷报告过滤两项软件工程任务中，GSE在OpenHands和mini-SWE-agent上均取得最佳精确率、召回率和F1值。相较现有技能演化方法，测试生成任务的精确率提升6.1%至34.1%、召回率提升31.8%至180.0%；误报过滤任务的精确率提升15.4%至96.4%、召回率提升13.1%至19.8%。在工业内部智能体上的部署使F1值进一步提升61.4%。

Conclusion: GSE通过全局建模技能关系、整合可复用能力并进行回放验证，有效提升了技能兼容性、泛化能力和演化稳定性。实验结果表明，该框架不仅适用于多种软件工程智能体，也具有较好的实际部署价值和跨场景有效性。

Abstract: Automated skill evolution enables Large Language Model (LLM) agents to continuously improve without expensive retraining. However, existing approaches typically treat skill evolution as a sequence of local updates, overlooking relationships among skills and often producing overfitted skill updates that fail to generalize across tasks. We propose GSE, a globalized skill evolution framework that jointly optimizes skill compatibility and skill generalization. To preserve consistency across the skill bank, GSE maintains a Skill Relation Graph (SRG) that explicitly models and co-evolves inter-skill relationships. To improve generalization, GSE performs cluster-based skill consolidation to abstract reusable capabilities from local updates and employs replay-driven verification to prevent overfitting and behavioral regressions. We evaluate GSE on two representative software engineering tasks: bug-revealing test generation and false-positive bug report filtering. Across two state-of-the-art coding agents, OpenHands and mini-SWE-agent, GSE consistently achieves the best precision, recall, and F1-score. Compared with existing evolution techniques, GSE improves precision and recall by 6.1%~34.1% and 31.8%~180.0% for test generation, and by 15.4%~96.4% and 13.1%~19.8% for false-positive filtering. Deployment on an internal industrial agent further yields a 61.4% improvement in F1-score, demonstrating the effectiveness and generalizability of GSE for evolving effective skills.

</details>


### [163] [Predicting Agile Success: The Critical Few Factors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06228)
*Ridewaan Hanslo,Maureen Tanner*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究采用PLS-SEM分析敏捷软件开发项目的关键成功因素，发现少数关键因素对项目成功具有显著影响，并据此构建了用于评估和衡量项目成功的新模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管敏捷项目通常优于传统软件开发项目，但整体成功率仍然不高，许多项目仍被判定为存在问题或失败。研究旨在识别敏捷实践者认知下影响项目成功的关键成功因素，并结合当代项目成功标准改进项目评价。

Method: 研究采用实证主义研究范式，通过调查敏捷软件开发从业者收集数据，使用SmartPLS开展偏最小二乘结构方程模型（PLS-SEM）分析，以检验各构念之间的关系及其对项目成功标准的影响。

Result: 分析结果表明，只有少数关键成功因素对敏捷项目成功产生显著贡献，相关假设及构念关系得到实证检验。研究进一步识别出可用于解释项目成功的关键因素组合。

Conclusion: 研究构建了一个新的敏捷项目成功评价模型，可帮助组织系统识别和衡量关键成功因素及项目成果，从而改进敏捷软件开发项目的管理与评估。

Abstract: While Agile projects are more successful than traditional software development project management approaches, their overall success rate remains relatively low, with a high percentage of projects still deemed challenged or failed. This low project success rate is attributed to projects not being rigorously evaluated against critical success factors (CSF) of Agile projects and contemporary project success criteria. To identify the CSF that contribute to Agile software development project success as perceived by Agile practitioners, this study used a positivist approach to investigate the CSF of Agile software development projects. PLS-SEM with SmartPLS was used to analyse the data and test the hypotheses to identify significant relationships between the constructs and project success criteria. This research found that a few critical factors significantly contribute to Agile project success. The study developed a novel model that can be used to evaluate and measure project success.

</details>


### [164] [Automatic Translation of Unstructured Requirements into Linear Temporal Logic through Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06287)
*Alexandra Newcomb,Omar Ochoa*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文评估六种通用大语言模型在将非结构化自然语言需求转换为线性时序逻辑（LTL）公式方面的能力。基于15条结构多样的需求和少样本提示，模型共生成450个候选公式，并通过语义正确性、pass@k及自一致性进行评估。结果表明，未经专门微调的LLM已具备较强的自动形式化潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全关键和任务关键系统的验证依赖数学上精确的形式化规格，而将自然语言需求人工转换为LTL公式通常困难、耗时且容易出错。因此，研究旨在评估现成LLM能否作为自然语言到形式规格之间的辅助桥梁。

Method: 研究测试六种现代LLM，采用少样本提示策略处理15条具有不同结构的自然语言需求。每个需求—模型组合独立生成五次，共获得450个LTL候选公式。评估指标包括人工语义正确性判断、pass@1、pass@3、pass@5，以及衡量随机生成结果在语法层面可重复性的自一致性指标。此外，研究还考察模型生成的自然语言解释，并讨论基于时间线的LTL可视化对非专家理解的辅助作用。

Result: 实验结果显示，通用LLM在未经任务专门微调的情况下，已经能够在自然语言到LTL任务上取得具有实际意义的表现。多次采样和pass@k评估表明，部分模型能够通过候选生成提高获得正确公式的概率；自一致性分析则反映出不同模型在输出稳定性方面存在差异。自然语言解释与可视化能够进一步改善公式的可理解性。

Conclusion: 现代LLM具备成为半自动形式化工作流前端助手的潜力，可协助将非结构化需求转换为LTL规格并降低形式化门槛。但其输出仍需人工语义审查和正式验证，不能直接替代领域专家或形式化验证工具。

Abstract: Automatically translating unstructured natural language requirements into formal specifications remains a challenge in requirements engineering and formal methods, particularly for safety- and mission-critical systems whose verification depends on mathematically precise specifications. This paper evaluates whether contemporary off-the-shelf Large Language Models (LLMs) can help bridge this gap by generating Linear Temporal Logic (LTL) formulas directly from unstructured requirements. The study examines six modern LLMs using a few-shot prompting strategy on a heterogeneous benchmark of 15 structurally varied requirements. Five independent generations were collected for each requirement-model pair, yielding 450 candidate LTL formulas in total. Performance was assessed through manual semantic evaluation, pass@k for k in {1, 3, 5}, and a self-consistency measure capturing syntactic reproducibility across stochastic trials. The results indicate that current general-purpose LLMs can achieve practically significant performance on the unstructured NL-to-LTL task without task-specific fine-tuning. The study also considers understandability for non-experts by pairing generated formulas with model-produced natural language explanations and discussing the complementary use of timeline-based LTL visualization. The findings suggest that modern LLMs are becoming viable front-end assistants for semi-automated formalization workflows.

</details>
